## `AFG-4762-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Scuba-Regulator-IP Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Vinyl Groove Micro-Scratch Microscopy & Optical Disc Reflectance

- **Standards:** `ISO 5725-1:1994`, `ISO/IEC 17025:2017`, `ANSI/ASTM E691-22 (Standard Practice for Conducting an Interlabo`, `IEC 61196-1-201:2016`

- **Role:** Treadmill-Inverter Mechanic
